Abstract: Scientific time series are central to scientific AI but are typically sparse, highly heterogeneous, and limited in scale, making unified representation learning particularly challenging. Meanwhile, foundation models pretrained on relevant time series domains such as audio, general time series, and brain signals contain rich knowledge, but their applicability to scientific signals remains underexplored. In this paper, we investigate the transferability and complementarity of foundation models from relevant time series domains, and study how to effectively leverage them to build a unified encoder for scientific time series. We first systematically evaluate relevant foundation models, showing the effectiveness of knowledge transfer to scientific tasks and their complementary strengths. Based on this observation, we propose STEP, a Scientific Time Series Encoder Pretraining framework via cross domain distillation. STEP introduces adaptive patching to handle extreme-length sequences and a statistics compensation scheme to accommodate diverse numerical scales. It further leverages cross-domain distillation to integrate knowledge from multiple foundation models into a unified encoder. By combining complementary representations across different domains, STEP learns general-purpose and transferable features tailored for scientific signals. Experiments on seven scientific time series tasks demonstrate that STEP provides both an effective structure and an effective pretraining paradigm, taking a STEP toward scientific time series representation learning.

</details>


### [151] [OCP: Orthogonal Constrained Projection for Sparse Scaling in Industrial Commodity Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18697)
*Chen Sun,Beilin Xu,Boheng Tan,Jiacheng Wang,Yuefeng Sun,Rite Bo,Ying He,Yaqiang Zang,Pinghua Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出正交约束投影方法优化商品ID词汇表表示，解决稀疏扩展中的低频信息干扰问题，提升推荐模型的扩展性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业商品推荐系统中，传统Item-Id词汇表在稀疏扩展时受到低频信息干扰，导致表示能力受限、表示坍缩，影响模型对海量商品集的表达能力和泛化性能。

Method: 提出正交约束投影方法，通过正交性约束反向传播流形，使学习到的嵌入表示与正交基对齐，确保高奇异熵，保留各向同性广义特征，同时抑制虚假相关性和对稀有商品的过拟合。

Result: OCP加速损失收敛并增强模型扩展性；在扩展密集层时保持性能提升；在京东大规模工业部署中，UCXR提升12.97%，GMV增长8.9%，验证了其对稀疏词汇表和密集架构扩展的有效性。

Conclusion: 正交约束投影方法有效解决了商品ID词汇表稀疏扩展中的表示质量问题，通过正交性约束优化嵌入表示，显著提升了推荐模型的扩展能力和工业应用效果。

Abstract: In industrial commodity recommendation systems, the representation quality of Item-Id vocabularies directly impacts the scalability and generalization ability of recommendation models. A key challenge is that traditional Item-Id vocabularies, when subjected to sparse scaling, suffer from low-frequency information interference, which restricts their expressive power for massive item sets and leads to representation collapse. To address this issue, we propose an Orthogonal Constrained Projection method to optimize embedding representation. By enforcing orthogonality, the projection constrains the backpropagation manifold, aligning the singular value spectrum of the learned embeddings with the orthogonal basis. This alignment ensures high singular entropy, thereby preserving isotropic generalized features while suppressing spurious correlations and overfitting to rare items. Empirical results demonstrate that OCP accelerates loss convergence and enhances the model's scalability; notably, it enables consistent performance gains when scaling up dense layers. Large-scale industrial deployment on JD.com further confirms its efficacy, yielding a 12.97% increase in UCXR and an 8.9% uplift in GMV, highlighting its robust utility for scaling up both sparse vocabularies and dense architectures.

</details>


### [152] [Off-Policy Learning with Limited Supply](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18702)
*Koichi Tanaka,Ren Kishimoto,Bushun Kawagishi,Yusuke Narita,Yasuo Yamamoto,Nobuyuki Shimizu,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对有限供给情境下上下文多臂老虎机的离策略学习方法OPLS，解决了传统贪婪方法在物品有限供给时性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用如优惠券分配和电子商务中，物品供给通常受到预算或库存限制。传统离策略学习假设无约束环境，在有限供给情境下，贪婪选择当前用户期望奖励最高的物品可能导致该物品过早耗尽，无法分配给未来可能产生更高期望奖励的用户，从而使传统方法在有限供给设置下变得次优。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的离策略学习方法OPLS（Off-Policy learning with Limited Supply）。与传统方法简单选择期望奖励最高的物品不同，OPLS关注相对于其他用户具有较高期望奖励的物品，从而实现有限供给物品的更有效分配。该方法基于理论分析，证明了在有限供给设置下必须存在性能更优的策略。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的实证结果表明，OPLS在有限供给的上下文多臂老虎机问题中优于现有的离策略学习方法。

Conclusion: 有限供给是现实应用中常见的重要约束，传统离策略学习方法在此情境下可能失效。OPLS方法通过考虑物品的相对期望奖励而非绝对期望奖励，实现了有限供给物品的更有效分配，在理论和实证上都表现出优越性能。

Abstract: We study off-policy learning (OPL) in contextual bandits, which plays a key role in a wide range of real-world applications such as recommendation systems and online advertising. Typical OPL in contextual bandits assumes an unconstrained environment where a policy can select the same item infinitely. However, in many practical applications, including coupon allocation and e-commerce, limited supply constrains items through budget limits on distributed coupons or inventory restrictions on products. In these settings, greedily selecting the item with the highest expected reward for the current user may lead to early depletion of that item, making it unavailable for future users who could potentially generate higher expected rewards. As a result, OPL methods that are optimal in unconstrained settings may become suboptimal in limited supply settings. To address the issue, we provide a theoretical analysis showing that conventional greedy OPL approaches may fail to maximize the policy performance, and demonstrate that policies with superior performance must exist in limited supply settings. Based on this insight, we introduce a novel method called Off-Policy learning with Limited Supply (OPLS). Rather than simply selecting the item with the highest expected reward, OPLS focuses on items with relatively higher expected rewards compared to the other users, enabling more efficient allocation of items with limited supply. Our empirical results on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that OPLS outperforms existing OPL methods in contextual bandit problems with limited supply.

</details>


### [153] [From ex(p) to poly: Gaussian Splatting with Polynomial Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18707)
*Joerg H. Mueller,Martin Winter,Markus Steinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种兼容现有数据集的高斯泼溅核函数改进方案，用多项式近似+ReLU替代原始指数核，提升计算效率4-15%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯泼溅(3DGS)的各种核函数改进与原始核优化的数据集不兼容，限制了广泛采用，需要一种既兼容现有数据集又能提升计算效率的替代方案

Method: 用多项式近似结合ReLU函数替代原始指数核，新核函数支持更激进的高斯函数剔除，提升计算效率

Result: 性能提升4-15%，图像质量影响可忽略，新核函数在不同3DGS实现中均有性能提升

Conclusion: 提出的多项式近似+ReLU核函数在保持与现有数据集兼容性的同时，显著提升计算效率，特别适合NPU硬件上的3DGS实现

Abstract: Recent advancements in Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have introduced various modifications to the original kernel, resulting in significant performance improvements. However, many of these kernel changes are incompatible with existing datasets optimized for the original Gaussian kernel, presenting a challenge for widespread adoption. In this work, we address this challenge by proposing an alternative kernel that maintains compatibility with existing datasets while improving computational efficiency. Specifically, we replace the original exponential kernel with a polynomial approximation combined with a ReLU function. This modification allows for more aggressive culling of Gaussians, leading to enhanced performance across different 3DGS implementations. Our results show a notable performance improvement of 4 to 15% with negligible impact on image quality. We also provide a detailed mathematical analysis of the new kernel and discuss its potential benefits for 3DGS implementations on NPU hardware.

</details>


### [154] [Are complicated loss functions necessary for teaching LLMs to reason?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18756)
*Gabriele Carrino,Andrea Sassella,Nicolo Brunello,Federico Toschi,Mark James Carman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出RGRA方法，通过简化GRPO去除PPO风格约束，仅保留组相对优势估计，在数学推理任务上实现更强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO方法虽然能提升LLM的推理和数学能力，但其复杂性引发疑问：是否所有组件都是必要的？需要系统分析GRPO各组件的作用，寻找更简洁有效的替代方案。

Method: 系统分析GRPO组件，发现两个关键结论：1) 需要包含负反馈；2) PPO风格约束非必需。基于此提出RGRA方法，保留组相对优势估计，去除PPO风格裁剪和策略比率项，采用简化的REINFORCE框架。

Result: 在标准数学基准测试中，RGRA有潜力实现比GRPO更强的性能，表明基于REINFORCE的简化方法能有效提升LLM的推理能力。

Conclusion: 更简单的REINFORCE方法可以有效地增强LLM的推理能力，为GRPO提供了更透明、高效的替代方案，简化了强化学习在LLM后训练中的应用。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) highlight the importance of post training techniques for improving reasoning and mathematical ability. Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has shown promise in this domain by combining group relative advantage estimation, PPO style clipping, and KL regularization. However, its complexity raises the question of whether all components are necessary for fostering reasoning behaviors. We conduct a systematic analysis of GRPO and identify two key findings: (1) incorporating negative feedback is essential training solely on actions above a baseline limits learning; and (2) PPO style constraints, such as policy ratio clipping, are not required to improve mathematical reasoning or performance. Building on these insights, we propose REINFORCE with Group Relative Advantage (RGRA), a simplified variant that retains group relative advantage estimation but removes PPO style clipping and policy ratio terms. Experiments across standard mathematical benchmarks indicate that RGRA has the potential to achieve stronger performance than GRPO. Our results suggest that simpler REINFORCE based approaches can effectively enhance reasoning in LLMs, offering a more transparent and efficient alternative to GRPO.

</details>


### [155] [Signals of Success and Struggle: Early Prediction and Physiological Signatures of Human Performance across Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18798)
*Yufei Cao,Penny Sweetser,Ziyu Chen,Xuanying Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用早期眼动和心电信号预测用户在游戏环境中的后期表现，融合模型达到0.86平衡准确率，揭示高低表现者的生理机制差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前瞻性预测用户表现对于及时识别任务困难用户至关重要。虽然眼动和心电信号广泛用于表征与表现相关的视觉行为和生理激活，但它们在早期预测和揭示表现差异生理机制方面的潜力尚未充分探索。

Method: 在具有自然复杂性的游戏环境中进行被试内实验，使用早期眼动和心电信号预测后期表现，并检查生理和自我报告的组间差异。采用眼动-心电融合模型和仅眼动模型进行预测分析。

Result: 眼动-心电融合模型达到0.86平衡准确率，仅眼动模型也显示出相当的预测能力。高表现者表现出针对性注视和调整的视觉采样，随着需求增加维持更稳定的心电激活，并具有更积极的情感体验。

Conclusion: 研究证明了从早期生理信号进行跨会话预测的可行性，为表现差异提供了可解释的见解，并促进未来的主动干预。高表现者的生理特征包括更有效的视觉策略和稳定的生理激活模式。

Abstract: User performance is crucial in interactive systems, capturing how effectively users engage with task execution. Prospectively predicting performance enables the timely identification of users struggling with task demands. While ocular and cardiac signals are widely used to characterise performance-relevant visual behaviour and physiological activation, their potential for early prediction and for revealing the physiological mechanisms underlying performance differences remains underexplored. We conducted a within-subject experiment in a game environment with naturally unfolding complexity, using early ocular and cardiac signals to predict later performance and to examine physiological and self-reported group differences. Results show that the ocular-cardiac fusion model achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.86, and the ocular-only model shows comparable predictive power. High performers exhibited targeted gaze and adjusted visual sampling, and sustained more stable cardiac activation as demands intensified, with a more positive affective experience. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of cross-session prediction from early physiology, providing interpretable insights into performance variation and facilitating future proactive intervention.

</details>


### [156] [Seasoning Generative Models for a Generalization Aftertaste](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18817)
*Hisham Husain,Valentin De Bortoli,Richard Nock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于判别器引导的生成模型精炼方法，通过扩展f-散度的强对偶性，为任何生成模型提供理论保证的改进框架，并分析了精炼后模型在泛化能力上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管判别器在训练生成模型（如GANs）中已证明有效，扩散模型也显示出判别器引导的优势，但缺乏统一的理论框架来理解判别器如何提升生成模型的泛化能力，以及如何将这种改进扩展到任意生成模型。

Method: 扩展f-散度的强对偶性结果，提出判别器引导的精炼方法，该方法可应用于任何生成模型。通过分析Rademacher复杂度，理论上证明精炼后模型在泛化能力上的提升。

Result: 提出的精炼方法能够提升生成模型的泛化能力，泛化差距的改进程度取决于用于精炼的判别器集合的Rademacher复杂度。该方法包含了Kim等人（2022）的得分扩散方法，但提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 该工作为现有方法提供了理论验证，提出了新算法的途径，并增进了对生成模型泛化能力的理解。判别器引导的精炼框架具有普适性，可应用于各种生成模型。

Abstract: The use of discriminators to train or fine-tune generative models has proven to be a rather successful framework. A notable example is Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) that minimize a loss incurred by training discriminators along with other paradigms that boost generative models via discriminators that satisfy weak learner constraints. More recently, even diffusion models have shown advantages with some kind of discriminator guidance. In this work, we extend a strong-duality result related to $f$-divergences which gives rise to a discriminator-guided recipe that allows us to \textit{refine} any generative model. We then show that the refined generative models provably improve generalization, compared to its non-refined counterpart. In particular, our analysis reveals that the gap in generalization is improved based on the Rademacher complexity of the discriminator set used for refinement. Our recipe subsumes a recently introduced score-based diffusion approach (Kim et al., 2022) that has shown great empirical success, however allows us to shed light on the generalization guarantees of this method by virtue of our analysis. Thus, our work provides a theoretical validation for existing work, suggests avenues for new algorithms, and contributes to our understanding of generalization in generative models at large.

</details>


### [157] [A Model Ensemble-Based Post-Processing Framework for Fairness-Aware Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18838)
*Zhouting Zhao,Tin Lok James Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型集成的后处理框架，在保持预测性能的同时提升机器学习模型的公平性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预测性能和公平性之间找到最优平衡是机器学习的基本挑战，需要一种广泛适用的解决方案

Method: 采用模型集成的后处理框架，不依赖特定模型内部结构，适用于多种学习任务、模型架构和公平性定义

Result: 在分类、回归和生存分析的广泛实验中，该框架有效提升了公平性，同时保持或仅轻微影响预测准确性

Conclusion: 提出的后处理框架为平衡预测性能和公平性提供了一种通用且有效的解决方案

Abstract: Striking an optimal balance between predictive performance and fairness continues to be a fundamental challenge in machine learning. In this work, we propose a post-processing framework that facilitates fairness-aware prediction by leveraging model ensembling. Designed to operate independently of any specific model internals, our approach is widely applicable across various learning tasks, model architectures, and fairness definitions. Through extensive experiments spanning classification, regression, and survival analysis, we demonstrate that the framework effectively enhances fairness while maintaining, or only minimally affecting, predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [158] [DriftGuard: Mitigating Asynchronous Data Drift in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18872)
*Yizhou Han,Di Wu,Blesson Varghese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DriftGuard是一个联邦持续学习框架，采用混合专家架构，通过分离共享参数和本地参数来高效适应异步数据漂移，减少83%的重新训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界联邦学习中，参与训练设备的数据分布会随时间演变，导致异步数据漂移。不同设备在不同时间向不同分布漂移。频繁重新训练在资源受限设备上计算成本高，而不频繁重新训练则导致漂移设备性能下降。

Method: 采用混合专家启发的架构，分离共享参数（捕获全局可转移知识）和本地参数（适应组特定分布）。提供两种互补的重新训练策略：1) 全局重新训练：当识别到系统范围漂移时更新共享参数；2) 组重新训练：通过MoE门控模式识别设备集群，选择性更新本地参数，无需共享原始数据。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型上的实验表明，DriftGuard达到或超过最先进精度，同时将总重新训练成本降低高达83%。在单位重新训练成本下的准确率最高，比最强基线提高达2.3倍。

Conclusion: DriftGuard通过混合专家架构有效解决了联邦学习中的异步数据漂移问题，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了重新训练成本，为资源受限环境下的联邦持续学习提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: In real-world Federated Learning (FL) deployments, data distributions on devices that participate in training evolve over time. This leads to asynchronous data drift, where different devices shift at different times and toward different distributions. Mitigating such drift is challenging: frequent retraining incurs high computational cost on resource-constrained devices, while infrequent retraining degrades performance on drifting devices. We propose DriftGuard, a federated continual learning framework that efficiently adapts to asynchronous data drift. DriftGuard adopts a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inspired architecture that separates shared parameters, which capture globally transferable knowledge, from local parameters that adapt to group-specific distributions. This design enables two complementary retraining strategies: (i) global retraining, which updates the shared parameters when system-wide drift is identified, and (ii) group retraining, which selectively updates local parameters for clusters of devices identified via MoE gating patterns, without sharing raw data. Experiments across multiple datasets and models show that DriftGuard matches or exceeds state-of-the-art accuracy while reducing total retraining cost by up to 83%. As a result, it achieves the highest accuracy per unit retraining cost, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 2.3x. DriftGuard is available for download from https://github.com/blessonvar/DriftGuard.

</details>


### [159] [Uniform a priori bounds and error analysis for the Adam stochastic gradient descent optimization method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18899)
*Steffen Dereich,Thang Do,Arnulf Jentzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次为Adam优化器在强凸随机优化问题上建立了无条件的误差分析，通过证明Adam的均匀先验界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam是深度学习中最流行的优化器，但即使在强凸随机优化问题上，也缺乏完整的误差分析。现有分析都是条件收敛的，依赖于Adam不发散到无穷大的假设。

Method: 为Adam建立了均匀先验界，从而能够提供无条件的误差分析，适用于一大类强凸随机优化问题。

Result: 首次为Adam优化器在强凸随机优化问题上提供了无条件的误差分析，证明了Adam的收敛性不依赖于不发散的假设。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了Adam优化器理论分析的空白，为Adam在强凸随机优化问题上的收敛性提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The adaptive moment estimation (Adam) optimizer proposed by Kingma & Ba (2014) is presumably the most popular stochastic gradient descent (SGD) optimization method for the training of deep neural networks (DNNs) in artificial intelligence (AI) systems. Despite its groundbreaking success in the training of AI systems, it still remains an open research problem to provide a complete error analysis of Adam, not only for optimizing DNNs but even when applied to strongly convex stochastic optimization problems (SOPs). Previous error analysis results for strongly convex SOPs in the literature provide conditional convergence analyses that rely on the assumption that Adam does not diverge to infinity but remains uniformly bounded. It is the key contribution of this work to establish uniform a priori bounds for Adam and, thereby, to provide -- for the first time -- an unconditional error analysis for Adam for a large class of strongly convex SOPs.

</details>


### [160] [Context Bootstrapped Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18953)
*Saaket Agashe,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,Ramana Kompella,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CBRL通过随机预置few-shot演示来增强RLVR训练，采用从高到零的课程学习策略，提升探索效率并强制模型内化推理模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR存在探索效率低的问题，特别是在需要获取新颖推理模式或领域特定知识的任务中，模型难以生成成功轨迹，导致学习信号微弱

Method: 提出上下文引导强化学习(CBRL)，在训练提示中随机预置few-shot演示，注入概率遵循从高到零的课程学习策略，早期引导探索，后期强制独立成功

Result: 在两个模型系列和五个Reasoning Gym任务上验证，CBRL一致提升成功率，提供更好的探索效率，且算法无关；在Q领域特定编程语言上展示了实际适用性

Conclusion: CBRL通过课程式few-shot演示注入有效解决了RLVR的探索效率问题，强制模型内化推理模式而非依赖测试时辅助，具有算法无关性和实际应用价值

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) suffers from exploration inefficiency, where models struggle to generate successful rollouts, resulting in minimal learning signal. This challenge is particularly severe for tasks that require the acquisition of novel reasoning patterns or domain-specific knowledge. To address this, we propose Context Bootstrapped Reinforcement Learning (CBRL), which augments RLVR training by stochastically prepending few-shot demonstrations to training prompts. The injection probability follows a curriculum that starts high to bootstrap early exploration, then anneals to zero so the model must ultimately succeed without assistance. This forces the policy to internalize reasoning patterns from the demonstrations rather than relying on them at test time. We validate CBRL across two model families and five Reasoning Gym tasks. Our results demonstrate that CBRL consistently improves success rate, provides better exploration efficiency, and is algorithm-agnostic. We further demonstrate CBRL's practical applicability on Q, a domain-specific programming language that diverges significantly from mainstream language conventions.

</details>


### [161] [BVSIMC: Bayesian Variable Selection-Guided Inductive Matrix Completion for Improved and Interpretable Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18957)
*Sijian Fan,Liyan Xiong,Dayuan Wang,Guoshuai Cai,Ray Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BVSIMC方法，通过贝叶斯变量选择从药物发现中的辅助特征中筛选重要特征，提高预测准确性和可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中辅助特征（如药物化学性质和疾病基因组信息）能显著提升预测性能，但这些特征通常相关性差异大、噪声多且高维，需要有效的方法进行特征筛选

Method: 提出贝叶斯变量选择引导的归纳矩阵补全方法，通过学习稀疏潜在嵌入实现辅助特征的变量选择

Result: 在模拟研究和两个药物发现应用（结核分枝杆菌耐药性预测和药物重定位）中，BVSIMC在预测性能上优于多种最先进方法，并能识别最具临床意义的辅助特征

Conclusion: BVSIMC通过贝叶斯变量选择有效处理药物发现中的高维噪声辅助特征，在提高预测准确性的同时增强模型可解释性，为药物发现提供有力工具

Abstract: Recent advances in drug discovery have demonstrated that incorporating side information (e.g., chemical properties about drugs and genomic information about diseases) often greatly improves prediction performance. However, these side features can vary widely in relevance and are often noisy and high-dimensional. We propose Bayesian Variable Selection-Guided Inductive Matrix Completion (BVSIMC), a new Bayesian model that enables variable selection from side features in drug discovery. By learning sparse latent embeddings, BVSIMC improves both predictive accuracy and interpretability. We validate our method through simulation studies and two drug discovery applications: 1) prediction of drug resistance in Mycobacterium tuberculosis, and 2) prediction of new drug-disease associations in computational drug repositioning. On both synthetic and real data, BVSIMC outperforms several other state-of-the-art methods in terms of prediction. In our two real examples, BVSIMC further reveals the most clinically meaningful side features.

</details>


### [162] [Maximum-Entropy Exploration with Future State-Action Visitation Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18965)
*Adrien Bolland,Gaspard Lambrechts,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的最大熵强化学习方法，通过内在奖励促进探索，并证明该方法具有理论保证和实际优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大熵强化学习通过最大化某些分布的熵来激励智能体探索状态和动作，但现有方法通常使用简单的熵函数。本文旨在开发一种基于状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的内在奖励方法，该方法具有更好的理论性质和实际性能。

Method: 提出内在奖励与未来时间步访问的状态-动作特征折扣分布的熵成比例。首先证明这些内在奖励的期望和是初始状态开始轨迹中状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的下界。其次证明该分布是收缩算子的不动点，因此可以离策略估计。通过实验验证新目标在特征探索方面的优势。

Result: 实验表明，新目标在单个轨迹内改善了特征的访问，但以略微减少不同轨迹间期望特征访问为代价（符合下界理论）。同时提高了纯探索智能体的收敛速度。在大多数基准测试中，控制性能与其他方法相似。

Conclusion: 基于状态-动作特征折扣分布熵的内在奖励方法具有理论保证，能够有效促进探索，特别是在单个轨迹内的特征探索方面表现优异，同时保持了与其他方法相当的控制性能。

Abstract: Maximum entropy reinforcement learning motivates agents to explore states and actions to maximize the entropy of some distribution, typically by providing additional intrinsic rewards proportional to that entropy function. In this paper, we study intrinsic rewards proportional to the entropy of the discounted distribution of state-action features visited during future time steps. This approach is motivated by two results. First, we show that the expected sum of these intrinsic rewards is a lower bound on the entropy of the discounted distribution of state-action features visited in trajectories starting from the initial states, which we relate to an alternative maximum entropy objective. Second, we show that the distribution used in the intrinsic reward definition is the fixed point of a contraction operator and can therefore be estimated off-policy. Experiments highlight that the new objective leads to improved visitation of features within individual trajectories, in exchange for slightly reduced visitation of features in expectation over different trajectories, as suggested by the lower bound. It also leads to improved convergence speed for learning exploration-only agents. Control performance remains similar across most methods on the considered benchmarks.

</details>


### [163] [Best-of-Both-Worlds Multi-Dueling Bandits: Unified Algorithms for Stochastic and Adversarial Preferences under Condorcet and Borda Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18972)
*S. Akash,Pratik Gajane,Jawar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了首个适用于随机和对抗环境的"两全其美"多决斗赌博机算法，针对Condorcet和Borda目标，无需预先知道所处环境类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多决斗赌博机在排名和推荐系统中自然出现，但一个基本问题尚未解决：是否存在单一算法能在随机和对抗环境中都表现最优，且无需知道面对的是哪种环境？

Method: 对于Condorcet设置，提出MetaDueling黑盒归约，将多路胜者反馈转换为无偏成对信号；对于Borda设置，提出AlgBorda算法，专门处理随机和对抗环境。

Result: MetaDueling结合Versatile-DB实现了O(√KT)对抗伪遗憾和实例最优O(∑_{i≠a*} logT/Δ_i)随机伪遗憾；AlgBorda实现了O(K²logKT + Klog²T + ∑_{i:Δ_i^B>0} KlogKT/(Δ_i^B)²)随机遗憾和O(K√TlogKT + K^{1/3}T^{2/3}(logK)^{1/3})对抗遗憾。

Conclusion: 首次证明了存在单一算法能在随机和对抗环境中都达到最优性能，无需预先知道环境类型，填补了多决斗赌博机领域的重要空白，并为Condorcet设置提供了匹配的下界。

Abstract: Multi-dueling bandits, where a learner selects $m \geq 2$ arms per round and observes only the winner, arise naturally in many applications including ranking and recommendation systems, yet a fundamental question has remained open: can a single algorithm perform optimally in both stochastic and adversarial environments, without knowing which regime it faces? We answer this affirmatively, providing the first best-of-both-worlds algorithms for multi-dueling bandits under both Condorcet and Borda objectives. For the Condorcet setting, we propose \texttt{MetaDueling}, a black-box reduction that converts any dueling bandit algorithm into a multi-dueling bandit algorithm by transforming multi-way winner feedback into an unbiased pairwise signal. Instantiating our reduction with \texttt{Versatile-DB} yields the first best-of-both-worlds algorithm for multi-dueling bandits: it achieves $O(\sqrt{KT})$ pseudo-regret against adversarial preferences and the instance-optimal $O\!\left(\sum_{i \neq a^\star} \frac{\log T}{Δ_i}\right)$ pseudo-regret under stochastic preferences, both simultaneously and without prior knowledge of the regime. For the Borda setting, we propose \AlgBorda, a stochastic-and-adversarial algorithm that achieves $O\left(K^2 \log KT + K \log^2 T + \sum_{i: Δ_i^{\mathrm{B}} > 0} \frac{K\log KT}{(Δ_i^{\mathrm{B}})^2}\right)$ regret in stochastic environments and $O\left(K \sqrt{T \log KT} + K^{1/3} T^{2/3} (\log K)^{1/3}\right)$ regret against adversaries, again without prior knowledge of the regime. We complement our upper bounds with matching lower bounds for the Condorcet setting. For the Borda setting, our upper bounds are near-optimal with respect to the lower bounds (within a factor of $K$) and match the best-known results in the literature.

</details>


### [164] [Foundations of Schrödinger Bridges for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18992)
*Sophia Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了薛定谔桥作为现代生成建模框架（扩散模型、基于分数的模型、流匹配）的统一理论基础，提供了从基本原理构建薛定谔桥的数学工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成建模框架（扩散模型、基于分数的模型、流匹配）的核心任务是通过概率空间中的随机路径将简单先验分布转换为复杂目标分布。薛定谔桥为这些方法提供了统一的理论基础，将问题框架化为在边际分布约束下确定最优随机桥，使其与预定义参考过程的熵偏差最小。

Method: 论文基于最优传输、随机控制和路径空间优化建立了薛定谔桥问题的数学基础，重点关注其动态表述及其与现代生成建模的直接联系。构建了从第一原理构建薛定谔桥的综合工具包。

Result: 展示了薛定谔桥构造如何产生广义和特定任务的计算方法，为理解现代生成建模提供了统一的理论框架。

Conclusion: 薛定谔桥为扩散模型、基于分数的模型和流匹配等现代生成建模方法提供了统一的理论基础，其动态表述和数学工具包为这些方法的理解和进一步发展奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: At the core of modern generative modeling frameworks, including diffusion models, score-based models, and flow matching, is the task of transforming a simple prior distribution into a complex target distribution through stochastic paths in probability space. Schrödinger bridges provide a unifying principle underlying these approaches, framing the problem as determining an optimal stochastic bridge between marginal distribution constraints with minimal-entropy deviations from a pre-defined reference process. This guide develops the mathematical foundations of the Schrödinger bridge problem, drawing on optimal transport, stochastic control, and path-space optimization, and focuses on its dynamic formulation with direct connections to modern generative modeling. We build a comprehensive toolkit for constructing Schrödinger bridges from first principles, and show how these constructions give rise to generalized and task-specific computational methods.

</details>


### [165] [When Differential Privacy Meets Wireless Federated Learning: An Improved Analysis for Privacy and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19040)
*Chen Yaoling,Liang Hao,Tu Xiaotong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对差分隐私无线联邦学习进行了全面分析，解决了隐私损失量化和收敛性分析中的关键问题，特别考虑了非凸目标函数、梯度裁剪和设备选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私无线联邦学习研究存在两个主要局限：1) 隐私损失量化方法不完善，基础性问题未解决；2) 收敛性分析依赖限制性凸性假设或忽略梯度裁剪影响。需要克服这些限制，为实际应用提供更准确的理论基础。

Method: 提出针对一般光滑非凸损失目标的DPWFL综合分析框架，明确纳入设备选择和mini-batch采样机制，考虑梯度裁剪的影响，建立隐私损失和收敛性的理论保证。

Result: 理论分析表明：1) 隐私损失可收敛至常数而非随迭代次数发散；2) 在梯度裁剪下建立了收敛保证；3) 推导出明确的隐私-效用权衡关系。数值实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 本文为差分隐私无线联邦学习提供了更全面的理论分析框架，解决了现有工作的局限性，为实际部署提供了更准确的隐私保护和收敛性保证。

Abstract: Differentially private wireless federated learning (DPWFL) is a promising framework for protecting sensitive user data. However, foundational questions on how to precisely characterize privacy loss remain open, and existing work is further limited by convergence analyses that rely on restrictive convexity assumptions or ignore the effect of gradient clipping. To overcome these issues, we present a comprehensive analysis of privacy and convergence for DPWFL with general smooth non-convex loss objectives. Our analysis explicitly incorporates both device selection and mini-batch sampling, and shows that the privacy loss can converge to a constant rather than diverge with the number of iterations. Moreover, we establish convergence guarantees with gradient clipping and derive an explicit privacy-utility trade-off. Numerical results validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [166] [Communication-Efficient and Robust Multi-Modal Federated Learning via Latent-Space Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19067)
*Mohamed Badi,Chaouki Ben Issaid,Mehdi Bennis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMFed：一种通信高效的多模态联邦学习框架，通过可学习的投影矩阵生成压缩的潜在表示，并使用潜在空间正则化器对齐不同客户端表示，在保持隐私和最小化通信成本的同时处理异构模态和模型架构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习（FL）支持分布式设备间的协作模型训练而无需共享原始数据，但将FL应用于多模态设置面临显著挑战。客户端通常拥有异构的模态和模型架构，这使得在保持隐私和最小化通信成本的同时高效对齐特征空间变得困难。

Method: 提出CoMFed框架：1）使用可学习的投影矩阵生成压缩的潜在表示；2）采用潜在空间正则化器对齐不同客户端的表示，提高跨模态一致性和对异常值的鲁棒性。

Result: 在人类活动识别基准测试上的实验表明，CoMFed以最小的开销实现了具有竞争力的准确性。

Conclusion: CoMFed框架有效解决了多模态联邦学习中的通信效率和特征对齐问题，在保持隐私的同时实现了良好的性能。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, but applying FL to multi-modal settings introduces significant challenges. Clients typically possess heterogeneous modalities and model architectures, making it difficult to align feature spaces efficiently while preserving privacy and minimizing communication costs. To address this, we introduce CoMFed, a Communication-Efficient Multi-Modal Federated Learning framework that uses learnable projection matrices to generate compressed latent representations. A latent-space regularizer aligns these representations across clients, improving cross-modal consistency and robustness to outliers. Experiments on human activity recognition benchmarks show that CoMFed achieves competitive accuracy with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [167] [On Optimizing Multimodal Jailbreaks for Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19127)
*Aravind Krishnan,Karolina Stańczak,Dietrich Klakow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAMA是一种针对语音语言模型的多模态越狱攻击方法，通过联合优化文本和音频模态，显著提高了越狱成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音语言模型继承了LLM的安全漏洞并扩展了攻击面，现有攻击大多为单模态，需要探索多模态越狱攻击的有效性

Method: 提出JAMA联合多模态优化框架，结合文本的贪婪坐标梯度和音频的投影梯度下降，同时扰动两种模态

Result: 在四种最先进的SLM和四种音频类型上，JAMA比单模态越狱率提高1.5到10倍，顺序近似方法使其速度提高4到6倍

Conclusion: 单模态安全性不足以保证SLM的鲁棒性，需要多模态安全防护

Abstract: As Spoken Language Models (SLMs) integrate speech and text modalities, they inherit the safety vulnerabilities of their LLM backbone and an expanded attack surface. SLMs have been previously shown to be susceptible to jailbreaking, where adversarial prompts induce harmful responses. Yet existing attacks largely remain unimodal, optimizing either text or audio in isolation. We explore gradient-based multimodal jailbreaks by introducing JAMA (Joint Audio-text Multimodal Attack), a joint multimodal optimization framework combining Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) for text and Projected Gradient Descent (PGD) for audio, to simultaneously perturb both modalities. Evaluations across four state-of-the-art SLMs and four audio types demonstrate that JAMA surpasses unimodal jailbreak rate by 1.5x to 10x. We analyze the operational dynamics of this joint attack and show that a sequential approximation method makes it 4x to 6x faster. Our findings suggest that unimodal safety is insufficient for robust SLMs. The code and data are available at https://repos.lsv.uni-saarland.de/akrishnan/multimodal-jailbreak-slm

</details>


### [168] [SHAPCA: Consistent and Interpretable Explanations for Machine Learning Models on Spectroscopy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19141)
*Mingxing Zhang,Nicola Rossberg,Simone Innocente,Katarzyna Komolibus,Rekha Gautam,Barry O'Sullivan,Luca Longo,Andrea Visentin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SHAPCA框架，结合主成分分析和SHAP解释方法，解决光谱数据高维共线性导致的模型可解释性问题，在原始输入空间提供稳定可解释的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光谱数据的高维度和强共线性给机器学习模型的可解释性带来根本挑战，导致特征重要性不稳定，影响在临床和安全关键场景中的可信度。现有特征提取方法虽然降低维度，但阻碍了预测结果与原始信号之间的关联。

Method: 提出SHAPCA可解释机器学习流程，结合主成分分析（用于降维）和SHAP可加性解释（用于事后解释），在原始输入空间提供解释，使从业者能够将预测与生物组分联系起来。

Result: 数值分析表明该方法结果具有可解释性，且在不同运行中表现出更高的一致性。框架支持全局和局部视角分析，既能揭示驱动整体模型行为的谱带，也能识别影响个体预测的实例特定特征。

Conclusion: SHAPCA框架有效解决了光谱数据可解释性挑战，提供稳定、一致且可追溯到原始信号的解释，有助于在化学和生物医学分析中建立对机器学习模型的信任。

Abstract: In recent years, machine learning models have been increasingly applied to spectroscopic datasets for chemical and biomedical analysis. For their successful adoption, particularly in clinical and safety-critical settings, professionals and researchers must be able to understand and trust the reasoning behind model predictions. However, the inherently high dimensionality and strong collinearity of spectroscopy data pose a fundamental challenge to model explainability. These properties not only complicate model training but also undermine the stability and consistency of explanations, leading to fluctuations in feature importance across repeated training runs. Feature extraction techniques have been used to reduce the input dimensionality; these new features hinder the connection between the prediction and the original signal. This study proposes SHAPCA, an explainable machine learning pipeline that combines Principal Component Analysis (for dimensionality reduction) and Shapely Additive exPlanations (for post hoc explanation) to provide explanations in the original input space, which a practitioner can interpret and link back to the biological components. The proposed framework enables analysis from both global and local perspectives, revealing the spectral bands that drive overall model behaviour as well as the instance-specific features that influence individual predictions. Numerical analysis demonstrated the interpretability of the results and greater consistency across different runs.

</details>


### [169] [DyMoE: Dynamic Expert Orchestration with Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient MoE Inference on Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19172)
*Yuegui Huang,Zhiyuan Fang,Weiqi Luo,Ruoyu Wu,Wuhui Chen,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DyMoE：一种动态混合精度量化框架，通过重要性感知优先级、深度自适应调度和前瞻预取技术，在资源受限的边缘设备上实现实时、精度保持的MoE推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在边缘平台实时推理面临内存占用大和I/O开销高的挑战，现有静态方法在延迟-精度权衡上过于僵化，且观察到专家重要性存在高度偏斜和深度依赖性

Method: 提出DyMoE框架，包含三个关键技术：1) 重要性感知优先级动态量化专家；2) 深度自适应调度保护关键层语义完整性；3) 前瞻预取重叠I/O延迟

Result: 在商用边缘硬件上，相比最先进的卸载基线，DyMoE将首词生成时间(TTFT)减少3.44-22.7倍，每输出词时间(TPOT)加速最高达14.58倍

Conclusion: DyMoE通过动态混合精度量化和优化调度策略，成功解决了MoE模型在边缘设备上的实时推理挑战，实现了精度保持的高性能边缘推理

Abstract: Despite the computational efficiency of MoE models, the excessive memory footprint and I/O overhead inherent in multi-expert architectures pose formidable challenges for real-time inference on resource-constrained edge platforms. While existing static methods struggle with a rigid latency-accuracy trade-off, we observe that expert importance is highly skewed and depth-dependent. Motivated by these insights, we propose DyMoE, a dynamic mixed-precision quantization framework designed for high-performance edge inference. Leveraging insights into expert importance skewness and depth-dependent sensitivity, DyMoE introduces: (1) importance-aware prioritization to dynamically quantize experts at runtime; (2) depth-adaptive scheduling to preserve semantic integrity in critical layers; and (3) look-ahead prefetching to overlap I/O stalls. Experimental results on commercial edge hardware show that DyMoE reduces Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) by 3.44x-22.7x and up to a 14.58x speedup in Time-Per-Output-Token (TPOT) compared to state-of-the-art offloading baselines, enabling real-time, accuracy-preserving MoE inference on resource-constrained edge devices.

</details>


### [170] [MIDST Challenge at SaTML 2025: Membership Inference over Diffusion-models-based Synthetic Tabular data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19185)
*Masoumeh Shafieinejad,Xi He,Mahshid Alinoori,John Jewell,Sana Ayromlou,Wei Pang,Veronica Chatrath,Garui Sharma,Deval Pandya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIDST挑战赛评估了扩散模型生成的合成表格数据在成员推理攻击下的隐私保护效果，开发了针对扩散模型的黑盒和白盒攻击方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据常被视为数据匿名化和隐私保护数据发布的理想解决方案，但扩散模型生成的表格数据在隐私攻击（特别是成员推理攻击）下的抵抗能力尚未得到充分探索

Method: MIDST挑战赛对扩散模型生成的合成表格数据进行定量隐私评估，针对混合数据类型单表和多关系约束表开发了专门的黑盒和白盒成员推理攻击方法

Result: 挑战赛开发了针对扩散模型的新型黑盒和白盒成员推理攻击方法，为全面评估合成表格数据的隐私效果提供了工具

Conclusion: MIDST挑战赛填补了扩散模型生成表格数据隐私评估的空白，通过开发专门攻击方法为合成数据的隐私保护效果提供了量化评估框架

Abstract: Synthetic data is often perceived as a silver-bullet solution to data anonymization and privacy-preserving data publishing. Drawn from generative models like diffusion models, synthetic data is expected to preserve the statistical properties of the original dataset while remaining resilient to privacy attacks. Recent developments of diffusion models have been effective on a wide range of data types, but their privacy resilience, particularly for tabular formats, remains largely unexplored. MIDST challenge sought a quantitative evaluation of the privacy gain of synthetic tabular data generated by diffusion models, with a specific focus on its resistance to membership inference attacks (MIAs). Given the heterogeneity and complexity of tabular data, multiple target models were explored for MIAs, including diffusion models for single tables of mixed data types and multi-relational tables with interconnected constraints. MIDST inspired the development of novel black-box and white-box MIAs tailored to these target diffusion models as a key outcome, enabling a comprehensive evaluation of their privacy efficacy. The MIDST GitHub repository is available at https://github.com/VectorInstitute/MIDST

</details>


### [171] [Improving RCT-Based Treatment Effect Estimation Under Covariate Mismatch via Calibrated Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19186)
*Amir Asiaee,Samhita Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALM方法通过嵌入对齐解决RCT与观察性研究间的协变量不匹配问题，避免插补，在非线性CATE估计中表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验(RCTs)是估计异质性治疗效应的金标准，但通常对检测效应异质性缺乏统计功效。大型观察性研究(OS)可以补充RCTs进行条件平均治疗效应(CATE)估计，但关键障碍是协变量不匹配：两个数据源测量不同且仅部分重叠的协变量。

Method: 提出CALM(Calibrated ALignment under covariate Mismatch)方法，通过学习嵌入将每个数据源的特征映射到共同的表示空间。将OS结果模型转移到RCT嵌入空间，并使用试验数据进行校准，同时保留随机化的因果识别性。

Result: 有限样本风险界限分解为对齐误差、结果模型复杂度和校准复杂度项，识别了嵌入对齐何时优于插补。在线性变体下，框架提供负迁移保护；神经变体在严重分布偏移下可能脆弱。在稀疏线性模型下，嵌入方法严格推广了插补。51个设置的模拟证实：(i)基于校准的方法在线性CATE中效果相当；(ii)神经嵌入变体在所有22个非线性设置中以较大优势胜出。

Conclusion: CALM通过嵌入对齐有效解决了RCT与观察性研究间的协变量不匹配问题，为CATE估计提供了优于传统插补的方法，特别是在非线性设置中表现突出。

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are the gold standard for estimating heterogeneous treatment effects, yet they are often underpowered for detecting effect heterogeneity. Large observational studies (OS) can supplement RCTs for conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimation, but a key barrier is covariate mismatch: the two sources measure different, only partially overlapping, covariates. We propose CALM (Calibrated ALignment under covariate Mismatch), which bypasses imputation by learning embeddings that map each source's features into a common representation space. OS outcome models are transferred to the RCT embedding space and calibrated using trial data, preserving causal identification from randomization. Finite-sample risk bounds decompose into alignment error, outcome-model complexity, and calibration complexity terms, identifying when embedding alignment outperforms imputation. Under the calibration-based linear variant, the framework provides protection against negative transfer; the neural variant can be vulnerable under severe distributional shift. Under sparse linear models, the embedding approach strictly generalizes imputation. Simulations across 51 settings confirm that (i) calibration-based methods are equivalent for linear CATEs, and (ii) the neural embedding variant wins all 22 nonlinear-regime settings with large margins.

</details>


### [172] [Robustness, Cost, and Attack-Surface Concentration in Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19204)
*Julian Allagan,Mohamed Elbakary,Zohreh Safari,Weizheng Gao,Gabrielle Morgan,Essence Morgan,Vladimir Deriglazov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究钓鱼网站检测器在对抗性特征操纵下的鲁棒性，发现模型在i.i.d.评估中表现优异，但对抗攻击下鲁棒性收敛，主要由特征经济学而非模型复杂度决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钓鱼检测器在独立同分布评估中达到近乎完美的准确率，但实际部署安全取决于对部署后特征操纵的鲁棒性。需要研究工程化特征检测器在对抗性攻击下的脆弱性。

Method: 提出成本感知的规避框架，建模离散、单调特征编辑，引入三个诊断指标：最小规避成本(MEC)、规避生存率S(B)和鲁棒性集中指数(RCI)。在UCI钓鱼网站基准(11,055实例，30个三元特征)上测试逻辑回归、随机森林、梯度提升树和XGBoost。

Result: 所有模型在静态评估中AUC≥0.979，但在预算化规避攻击下鲁棒性收敛：全特征时中位MEC为2，超过80%的最小成本规避集中在三个低成本表面特征。特征限制仅在移除所有主导低成本转换时改善鲁棒性。严格成本计划下，基础设施偏向特征集对集成模型有17-19%不可行质量，而可规避实例的中位MEC保持不变。

Conclusion: 钓鱼检测的对抗鲁棒性由特征经济学而非模型复杂度主导。如果正确检测的钓鱼实例中有正比例可通过最小成本c_min的单一特征转换规避，则任何分类器都无法在不修改特征表示或成本模型的情况下提高相应的MEC分位数。

Abstract: Phishing detectors built on engineered website features attain near-perfect accuracy under i.i.d.\ evaluation, yet deployment security depends on robustness to post-deployment feature manipulation. We study this gap through a cost-aware evasion framework that models discrete, monotone feature edits under explicit attacker budgets. Three diagnostics are introduced: minimal evasion cost (MEC), the evasion survival rate $S(B)$, and the robustness concentration index (RCI).
  On the UCI Phishing Websites benchmark (11\,055 instances, 30 ternary features), Logistic Regression, Random Forests, Gradient Boosted Trees, and XGBoost all achieve $\mathrm{AUC}\ge 0.979$ under static evaluation. Under budgeted sanitization-style evasion, robustness converges across architectures: the median MEC equals 2 with full features, and over 80\% of successful minimal-cost evasions concentrate on three low-cost surface features. Feature restriction improves robustness only when it removes all dominant low-cost transitions. Under strict cost schedules, infrastructure-leaning feature sets exhibit 17-19\% infeasible mass for ensemble models, while the median MEC among evadable instances remains unchanged. We formalize this convergence: if a positive fraction of correctly detected phishing instances admit evasion through a single feature transition of minimal cost $c_{\min}$, no classifier can raise the corresponding MEC quantile above $c_{\min}$ without modifying the feature representation or cost model. Adversarial robustness in phishing detection is governed by feature economics rather than model complexity.

</details>


### [173] [Online Learning and Equilibrium Computation with Ranking Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19221)
*Mingyang Liu,Yongshan Chen,Zhiyuan Fan,Gabriele Farina,Asuman Ozdaglar,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究仅基于排序反馈的在线学习，分析了瞬时效用和时间平均效用两种排序机制，在完全信息和赌博机设置下的遗憾界限，并应用于博弈均衡计算和LLM路由任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线学习算法依赖数值效用反馈，但在人机交互应用或隐私受限场景中可能不可用。本文研究仅基于动作排序反馈的在线学习模型，探索在更受限反馈下的学习能力。

Method: 提出两种排序反馈机制：基于瞬时效用的排序和基于时间平均效用的排序，分别在完全信息和赌博机反馈设置下分析。使用外部遗憾度量，证明在某些条件下亚线性遗憾不可能，在效用序列具有亚线性总变差假设下开发新算法实现亚线性遗憾。

Result: 1) 瞬时效用排序反馈下一般无法实现亚线性遗憾；2) 在Plackett-Luce模型温度足够小时，时间平均效用排序反馈也无法实现亚线性遗憾；3) 在效用序列具有亚线性总变差假设下，新算法能实现亚线性遗憾；4) 完全信息时间平均效用排序反馈下无需额外假设；5) 应用于博弈论可得到近似粗相关均衡；6) 在在线大语言模型路由任务中验证有效性。

Conclusion: 仅基于排序反馈的在线学习在特定条件下可实现亚线性遗憾，为隐私敏感和人机交互场景提供了新的学习框架，在博弈均衡计算和实际应用中具有价值。

Abstract: Online learning in arbitrary, and possibly adversarial, environments has been extensively studied in sequential decision-making, and it is closely connected to equilibrium computation in game theory. Most existing online learning algorithms rely on \emph{numeric} utility feedback from the environment, which may be unavailable in human-in-the-loop applications and/or may be restricted by privacy concerns. In this paper, we study an online learning model in which the learner only observes a \emph{ranking} over a set of proposed actions at each timestep. We consider two ranking mechanisms: rankings induced by the \emph{instantaneous} utility at the current timestep, and rankings induced by the \emph{time-average} utility up to the current timestep, under both \emph{full-information} and \emph{bandit} feedback settings. Using the standard external-regret metric, we show that sublinear regret is impossible with instantaneous-utility ranking feedback in general. Moreover, when the ranking model is relatively deterministic, \emph{i.e.}, under the Plackett-Luce model with a temperature that is sufficiently small, sublinear regret is also impossible with time-average utility ranking feedback. We then develop new algorithms that achieve sublinear regret under the additional assumption that the utility sequence has sublinear total variation. Notably, for full-information time-average utility ranking feedback, this additional assumption can be removed. As a consequence, when all players in a normal-form game follow our algorithms, repeated play yields an approximate coarse correlated equilibrium. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of our algorithms in an online large-language-model routing task.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Final Report for the Workshop on Robotics & AI in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18130)
*Juan P Wachs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 2025年CARE研讨会召集医疗机器人AI领域专家，讨论建立国家医疗AI与机器人卓越中心，解决数据、评估、监管等关键障碍，推动智能机器人系统在临床转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域对机器人技术和人工智能的需求日益增长，但面临数据可用性、标准化评估方法、监管路径和人员培训等关键障碍，阻碍了智能机器人系统在手术、诊断、康复和辅助等临床环境中的部署。

Method: 通过2025年12月1日在印第安纳波利斯举办的CARE研讨会，汇集研究人员、临床医生、行业创新者和联邦利益相关者，通过主题演讲、小组讨论和分组会议等形式，共同探讨医疗机器人AI的发展愿景和挑战。

Result: 研讨会达成广泛共识，迫切需要建立国家医疗AI与机器人卓越中心(CARE)，确定了优先研究方向：人机协作、可信自主性、仿真与数字孪生、多模态传感以及生成式AI在临床工作流程中的伦理整合。

Conclusion: 医疗机器人AI具有改善精准度、减轻医护人员负担、扩大专科护理可及性、提升患者预后的变革潜力，特别是在资源匮乏地区和高风险手术领域，需要建立国家级协调机制来加速临床转化。

Abstract: The CARE Workshop on Robotics and AI in Medicine, held on December 1, 2025 in Indianapolis, convened leading researchers, clinicians, industry innovators, and federal stakeholders to shape a national vision for advancing robotics and artificial intelligence in healthcare. The event highlighted the accelerating need for coordinated research efforts that bridge engineering innovation with real clinical priorities, emphasizing safety, reliability, and translational readiness with an emphasis on the use of robotics and AI to achieve this readiness goal.
  Across keynotes, panels, and breakout sessions, participants underscored critical gaps in data availability, standardized evaluation methods, regulatory pathways, and workforce training that hinder the deployment of intelligent robotic systems in surgical, diagnostic, rehabilitative, and assistive contexts. Discussions emphasized the transformative potential of AI enabled robotics to improve precision, reduce provider burden, expand access to specialized care, and enhance patient outcomes particularly in undeserved regions and high risk procedural domains. Special attention was given to austere settings, disaster and relief and military settings.
  The workshop demonstrated broad consensus on the urgency of establishing a national Center for AI and Robotic Excellence in medicine (CARE). Stakeholders identified priority research thrusts including human robot collaboration, trustworthy autonomy, simulation and digital twins, multi modal sensing, and ethical integration of generative AI into clinical workflows. Participants also articulated the need for high quality datasets, shared test beds, autonomous surgical systems, clinically grounded benchmarks, and sustained interdisciplinary training mechanisms.

</details>


### [175] [GoalVLM: VLM-driven Object Goal Navigation for Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18210)
*MoniJesu James,Amir Atef Habel,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserokou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoalVLM是一个用于零样本开放词汇目标导航的多智能体框架，通过集成视觉语言模型实现无需任务特定训练的自由形式语言目标导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标导航方法局限于地面机器人和封闭词汇集，现有多智能体方法依赖于预计算的概率图且无法泛化到测试时的新目标。

Method: 集成视觉语言模型到决策循环中，使用SAM3进行文本提示检测和分割，SpaceOM进行空间推理。构建BEV语义地图，通过Goal Projector将检测结果反向投影到地图中，约束引导推理层通过结构化提示链评估边界。

Result: 在GOAT-Bench val_unseen数据集上，使用2个智能体达到55.8%子任务成功率和18.3%SPL，与最先进方法竞争且无需任务特定训练。

Conclusion: GoalVLM展示了零样本开放词汇目标导航的可行性，VLM引导的边界推理和深度投影目标定位是有效方法，为无需训练的多智能体导航提供了新途径。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation has traditionally been limited to ground robots with closed-set object vocabularies. Existing multi-agent approaches depend on precomputed probabilistic graphs tied to fixed category sets, precluding generalization to novel goals at test time.
  We present GoalVLM, a cooperative multi-agent framework for zero-shot, open-vocabulary object navigation. GoalVLM integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) directly into the decision loop, SAM3 for text-prompted detection and segmentation, and SpaceOM for spatial reasoning, enabling agents to interpret free-form language goals and score frontiers via zero-shot semantic priors without retraining. Each agent builds a BEV semantic map from depth-projected voxel splatting, while a Goal Projector back-projects detections through calibrated depth into the map for reliable goal localization. A constraint-guided reasoning layer evaluates frontiers through a structured prompt chain (scene captioning, room-type classification, perception gating, multi-frontier ranking), injecting commonsense priors into exploration.
  We evaluate GoalVLM on GOAT-Bench val_unseen (360 multi-subtask episodes, 1032 sequential object-goal subtasks, HM3D scenes), where each episode requires navigating to a chain of 5-7 open-vocabulary targets. GoalVLM with N=2 agents achieves 55.8% subtask SR and 18.3% SPL, competitive with state-of-the-art methods while requiring no task-specific training. Ablation studies confirm the contributions of VLM-guided frontier reasoning and depth-projected goal localization.

</details>


### [176] [ReDAG-RT: Global Rate-Priority Scheduling for Real-Time Multi-DAG Execution in ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18238)
*Md. Mehedi Hasan,Rafid Mostafiz,Bikash Kumar Paul,Md. Abir Hossain,Ziaur Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReDAGRT：一种用于未修改ROS 2的用户空间全局调度框架，通过速率优先级驱动的全局就绪队列实现确定性多DAG执行，减少截止时间错过率和响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 默认ROS 2执行器采用尽力而为调度，缺乏跨DAG优先级强制执行，导致回调争用、结构优先级反转和截止时间不稳定，限制了在时间关键和安全关键系统中的部署。

Method: 提出ReDAGRT框架，引入速率优先级驱动的全局就绪队列，按激活速率排序回调，强制执行每DAG并发边界，缓解跨图优先级反转，无需修改ROS 2 API、执行器接口或底层操作系统调度器。

Result: 在ROS 2 Humble环境中实验显示，相比SingleThreadedExecutor和MultiThreadedExecutor，ReDAGRT减少截止时间错过率29.7%，降低99%百分位响应时间42.9%，在可比利用率下比MultiThreadedExecutor提升13.7%。非对称每DAG并发边界进一步减少干扰40.8%。

Conclusion: 确定性可分析的多DAG调度可在ROS 2用户空间执行层完全实现，为安全关键系统中的实时机器人中间件提供实用基础。

Abstract: ROS 2 has become a dominant middleware for robotic systems, where perception, estimation, planning, and control pipelines are structured as directed acyclic graphs of callbacks executed under a shared executor. However, default ROS 2 executors use best-effort dispatch without cross-DAG priority enforcement, leading to callback contention, structural priority inversion, and deadline instability under concurrent workloads. These limitations restrict deployment in time-critical and safety-sensitive cyber-physical systems. This paper presents ReDAGRT, a user-space global scheduling framework for deterministic multi-DAG execution in unmodified ROS 2. The framework introduces a Rate-Priority driven global ready queue that orders callbacks by activation rate, enforces per-DAG concurrency bounds, and mitigates cross-graph priority inversion without modifying the ROS 2 API, executor interface, or underlying operating system scheduler. We formalize a multi-DAG task model for ROS 2 callback pipelines and analyze cross-DAG interference under Rate-Priority scheduling. Response-time recurrences and schedulability conditions are derived within classical Rate-Monotonic theory. Experiments in a ROS 2 Humble environment compare ReDAGRT against SingleThreadedExecutor and MultiThreadedExecutor using synthetic multi-DAG workloads. Results show up to 29.7 percent reduction in deadline miss rate, 42.9 percent reduction in 99th percentile response time, and 13.7 percent improvement over MultiThreadedExecutor under comparable utilization. Asymmetric per-DAG concurrency bounds further reduce interference by 40.8 percent. These results demonstrate that deterministic and analyzable multi-DAG scheduling can be achieved entirely in the ROS 2 user-space execution layer, providing a practical foundation for real-time robotic middleware in safety-critical systems.

</details>


### [177] [Rapid Adaptation of Particle Dynamics for Generalized Deformable Object Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18246)
*Bohan Wu,Roberto Martín-Martín,Li Fei-Fei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPiD：一种将快速运动适应（RMA）扩展到可变形物体操作的两阶段方法，通过从特权信息学习动态嵌入，然后从非特权观察中推断该嵌入，实现真实世界中的可变形物体移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在未知动力学条件下学习操作可变形物体的挑战。现有RMA方法主要处理刚体动力学（如质量和位置），但可变形物体的动力学还包括形状变化，需要新的方法来适应未知的可变形物体动力学。

Method: RAPiD采用两阶段方法：1）在仿真中学习基于物体动态嵌入的视觉运动策略，嵌入从特权信息（如质量、地面真实粒子位置）编码；2）学习从非特权信息（机器人视觉观察和动作）推断该嵌入，使策略能迁移到真实世界。

Result: 在具有22自由度的移动机械手上，RAPiD在两个基于视觉的可变形物体移动操作任务中实现了超过80%的成功率，适用于各种物体动力学、类别和实例。

Conclusion: 通过利用仿真中的地面真实粒子位置捕获形状变化，成功将RMA扩展到可变形物体操作，实现了从仿真到真实世界的有效迁移，解决了可变形物体操作中的未知动力学适应问题。

Abstract: We address the challenge of learning to manipulate deformable objects with unknown dynamics. In non-rigid objects, the dynamics parameters define how they react to interactions -- how they stretch, bend, compress, and move -- and they are critical to determining the optimal actions to perform a manipulation task successfully. In other robotic domains, such as legged locomotion and in-hand rigid object manipulation, state-of-the-art approaches can handle unknown dynamics using Rapid Motor Adaptation (RMA). Through a supervised procedure in simulation that encodes each rigid object's dynamics, such as mass and position, these approaches learn a policy that conditions actions on a vector of latent dynamic parameters inferred from sequences of state-actions. However, in deformable object manipulation, the object's dynamics not only includes its mass and position, but also how the shape of the object changes. Our key insight is that the recent ground-truth particle positions of a deformable object in simulation capture changes in the object's shape, making it possible to extend RMA to deformable object manipulation. This key insight allows us to develop RAPiD, a two-phase method that learns to perform real-robot deformable object mobile manipulation by: 1) learning a visuomotor policy conditioned on the object's dynamics embedding, which is encoded from the object's privileged information in simulation, such as its mass and ground-truth particle positions, and 2) learning to infer this embedding using non-privileged information instead, such as robot visual observations and actions, so that the learned policy can transfer to the real world. On a mobile manipulator with 22 degrees of freedom, RAPiD enables over 80%+ success rates across two vision-based deformable object mobile manipulation tasks in the real world, under various object dynamics, categories, and instances.

</details>


### [178] [Manufacturing Micro-Patterned Surfaces with Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18260)
*Annalisa T. Taylor,Malachi Landis,Ping Guo,Todd D. Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多机器人系统使用遍历控制算法协调制造微图案表面，通过压缩轨迹历史通信实现复杂覆盖任务分工，降低金属表面摩擦系数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前微图案表面制造技术因高成本设备和低效的扫描覆盖方法而无法实现规模化生产，需要开发可扩展的制造方案

Method: 使用配备图案化工具的多机器人系统，采用遍历控制算法通过分布指定覆盖目标，机器人通过压缩轨迹历史表示进行通信协调

Result: 机器人能够通过通信协调完成复杂覆盖任务分工，在仿真和实验中验证了可行性，制造的微图案表面能降低金属摩擦系数

Conclusion: 分布式多机器人系统能够协调制造先前无法规模化生产的微图案产品，为表面工程提供了可扩展的制造方案

Abstract: Applying micro-patterns to surfaces has been shown to impart useful physical properties such as drag reduction and hydrophobicity. However, current manufacturing techniques cannot produce micro-patterned surfaces at scale due to high-cost machinery and inefficient coverage techniques such as raster-scanning. In this work, we use multiple robots, each equipped with a patterning tool, to manufacture these surfaces. To allow these robots to coordinate during the patterning task, we use the ergodic control algorithm, which specifies coverage objectives using distributions. We demonstrate that robots can divide complicated coverage objectives by communicating compressed representations of their trajectory history both in simulations and experimental trials. Further, we show that robot-produced patterning can lower the coefficient of friction of metallic surfaces. This work demonstrates that distributed multi-robot systems can coordinate to manufacture products that were previously unrealizable at scale.

</details>


### [179] [SG-CoT: An Ambiguity-Aware Robotic Planning Framework using Scene Graph Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18271)
*Akshat Rana,Peeyush Agarwal,K. P. S. Rana,Amarjit Malhotra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-CoT是一个两阶段框架，通过让LLM迭代查询场景图来检测和澄清机器人规划中的歧义问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 歧义性是大型语言模型作为机器人规划器时面临的主要挑战，需要提高在模糊情况下的规划可靠性

Method: 采用两阶段框架：1) 从输入观测构建结构化场景图表示；2) LLM通过检索函数查询相关场景图部分，并识别歧义源向用户提问澄清

Result: SG-CoT在问题准确率上至少提升10%，单智能体成功率至少提升4%，多智能体环境成功率至少提升15%，显著优于现有方法

Conclusion: SG-CoT通过场景图增强的思维链方法有效提高了机器人规划在模糊情况下的可靠性和泛化能力

Abstract: Ambiguity poses a major challenge to large language models (LLMs) used as robotic planners. In this letter, we present Scene Graph-Chain-of-Thought (SG-CoT), a two-stage framework where LLMs iteratively query a scene graph representation of the environment to detect and clarify ambiguities. First, a structured scene graph representation of the environment is constructed from input observations, capturing objects, their attributes, and relationships with other objects. Second, the LLM is equipped with retrieval functions to query portions of the scene graph that are relevant to the provided instruction. This grounds the reasoning process of the LLM in the observation, increasing the reliability of robotic planners under ambiguous situations. SG-CoT also allows the LLM to identify the source of ambiguity and pose a relevant disambiguation question to the user or another robot. Extensive experimentation demonstrates that SG-CoT consistently outperforms prior methods, with a minimum of 10% improvement in question accuracy and a minimum success rate increase of 4% in single-agent and 15% in multi-agent environments, validating its effectiveness for more generalizable robot planning.

</details>


### [180] [Sparse3DTrack: Monocular 3D Object Tracking Using Sparse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18298)
*Nikhil Gosala,B. Ravi Kiran,Senthil Yogamani,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个稀疏监督的单目3D目标跟踪框架，通过将任务分解为2D查询匹配和3D几何估计两个子问题，利用时空一致性从稀疏标注生成高质量伪标签，显著提升跟踪性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的单目3D目标跟踪方法需要密集的3D标注，这在长视频序列中获取成本高昂且难以扩展。为了解决这一根本限制，研究者提出了稀疏监督框架

Method: 将任务分解为两个顺序子问题：1) 2D查询匹配，2) 3D几何估计。两个组件都利用图像序列的时空一致性来增强稀疏标注样本，学习丰富的2D和3D场景表示。通过这些学习到的线索，模型自动生成整个视频的高质量3D伪标签，将稀疏监督转化为密集的3D跟踪标注

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法显著提高了跟踪性能，在每条轨迹最多使用4个真实标注的情况下，性能提升高达15.50个百分点

Conclusion: 提出的稀疏监督框架有效解决了单目3D目标跟踪中标注成本高的问题，通过利用时空一致性从稀疏标注生成密集伪标签，使现有全监督跟踪器能够在极端标签稀疏条件下有效工作

Abstract: Monocular 3D object tracking aims to estimate temporally consistent 3D object poses across video frames, enabling autonomous agents to reason about scene dynamics. However, existing state-of-the-art approaches are fully supervised and rely on dense 3D annotations over long video sequences, which are expensive to obtain and difficult to scale. In this work, we address this fundamental limitation by proposing the first sparsely supervised framework for monocular 3D object tracking. Our approach decomposes the task into two sequential sub-problems: 2D query matching and 3D geometry estimation. Both components leverage the spatio-temporal consistency of image sequences to augment a sparse set of labeled samples and learn rich 2D and 3D representations of the scene. Leveraging these learned cues, our model automatically generates high-quality 3D pseudolabels across entire videos, effectively transforming sparse supervision into dense 3D track annotations. This enables existing fully-supervised trackers to effectively operate under extreme label sparsity. Extensive experiments on the KITTI and nuScenes datasets demonstrate that our method significantly improves tracking performance, achieving an improvement of up to 15.50 p.p. while using at most four ground truth annotations per track.

</details>


### [181] [Proprioceptive-only State Estimation for Legged Robots with Set-Coverage Measurements of Learned Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18308)
*Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Ioannis Poulakakis,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于集合覆盖噪声表征的腿式机器人本体感知状态估计框架，避免高斯噪声假设，在有限训练数据下保持估计一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于高斯噪声假设的本体感知状态估计方法在训练数据有限时容易失效，导致估计不一致和漂移问题。需要一种不依赖特定分布假设的鲁棒估计方法。

Method: 提出使用集合覆盖语句表征测量噪声的非分布假设方法，开发了与高斯滤波器系统集成的实用计算高效方案，通过关节测量历史推断系统动态。

Result: 在仿真和两个真实世界四足机器人数据集上验证，相比高斯基线方法，所提方法保持一致性且在真实噪声场景下不易漂移。

Conclusion: 基于集合覆盖噪声表征的本体感知状态估计框架在有限训练数据下比传统高斯方法更鲁棒，能够提供一致且无漂移的导航测量。

Abstract: Proprioceptive-only state estimation is attractive for legged robots since it is computationally cheaper and is unaffected by perceptually degraded conditions. The history of joint-level measurements contains rich information that can be used to infer the dynamics of the system and subsequently produce navigational measurements. Recent approaches produce these estimates with learned measurement models and fuse with IMU data, under a Gaussian noise assumption. However, this assumption can easily break down with limited training data and render the estimates inconsistent and potentially divergent. In this work, we propose a proprioceptive-only state estimation framework for legged robots that characterizes the measurement noise using set-coverage statements that do not assume any distribution. We develop a practical and computationally inexpensive method to use these set-coverage measurements with a Gaussian filter in a systematic way. We validate the approach in both simulation and two real-world quadrupedal datasets. Comparison with the Gaussian baselines shows that our proposed method remains consistent and is not prone to drift under real noise scenarios.

</details>


### [182] [DriveVLM-RL: Neuroscience-Inspired Reinforcement Learning with Vision-Language Models for Safe and Deployable Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18315)
*Zilin Huang,Zihao Sheng,Zhengyang Wan,Yansong Qu,Junwei You,Sicong Jiang,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveVLM-RL：一种神经科学启发的双通路架构，将视觉语言模型集成到强化学习中，用于安全可部署的自动驾驶，通过离线训练实现实时可行性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习方法依赖手工设计的奖励或稀疏碰撞信号，无法捕捉安全驾驶所需的丰富上下文理解，且在实际环境中不可避免地进行不安全探索。视觉语言模型具有语义理解能力，但其高推理延迟和幻觉问题阻碍了实时车辆控制应用。

Method: 提出DriveVLM-RL框架，采用双通路架构：静态通路使用CLIP对比语言目标进行连续空间安全评估；动态通路使用轻量级检测器和大型VLM进行注意力门控多帧语义风险推理。通过分层奖励合成机制融合语义信号与车辆状态，异步训练管道解耦昂贵的VLM推理与环境交互。所有VLM组件仅在离线训练中使用，部署时移除以确保实时可行性。

Result: 在CARLA模拟器中的实验显示，在碰撞避免、任务成功率和多样化交通场景泛化方面有显著改进，包括在没有显式碰撞惩罚设置下的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DriveVLM-RL为将基础模型集成到自动驾驶中提供了一个实用范式，且不损害实时可行性，展示了神经科学启发的双通路架构在安全决策中的有效性。

Abstract: Ensuring safe decision-making in autonomous vehicles remains a fundamental challenge despite rapid advances in end-to-end learning approaches. Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) methods rely on manually engineered rewards or sparse collision signals, which fail to capture the rich contextual understanding required for safe driving and make unsafe exploration unavoidable in real-world settings. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) offer promising semantic understanding capabilities; however, their high inference latency and susceptibility to hallucination hinder direct application to real-time vehicle control. To address these limitations, this paper proposes DriveVLM-RL, a neuroscience-inspired framework that integrates VLMs into RL through a dual-pathway architecture for safe and deployable autonomous driving. The framework decomposes semantic reward learning into a Static Pathway for continuous spatial safety assessment using CLIP-based contrasting language goals, and a Dynamic Pathway for attention-gated multi-frame semantic risk reasoning using a lightweight detector and a large VLM. A hierarchical reward synthesis mechanism fuses semantic signals with vehicle states, while an asynchronous training pipeline decouples expensive VLM inference from environment interaction. All VLM components are used only during offline training and are removed at deployment, ensuring real-time feasibility. Experiments in the CARLA simulator show significant improvements in collision avoidance, task success, and generalization across diverse traffic scenarios, including strong robustness under settings without explicit collision penalties. These results demonstrate that DriveVLM-RL provides a practical paradigm for integrating foundation models into autonomous driving without compromising real-time feasibility. Demo video and code are available at: https://zilin-huang.github.io/DriveVLM-RL-website/

</details>


### [183] [ManiDreams: An Open-Source Library for Robust Object Manipulation via Uncertainty-aware Task-specific Intuitive Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18336)
*Gaotian Wang,Kejia Ren,Andrew S. Morgan,Kaiyu Hang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiDreams是一个用于不确定性感知操作规划的模块化框架，通过可组合的分布状态表示、后端无关的动力学预测和声明式约束规范，在规划循环中集成不确定性处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力学模型主要关注最小化预测误差，但现实世界操作本质上是不确定的。鲁棒操作需要将不确定性表示、传播和约束集成到规划循环中，而不仅仅是在训练中抑制不确定性。

Method: 提出ManiDreams框架，包含三个核心抽象：分布状态表示、后端无关的动力学预测、声明式约束规范。通过"采样-预测-约束"循环包装任何基础策略，在候选动作评估中考虑分布结果，处理感知、参数和结构三种不确定性来源。

Result: 在ManiSkill任务上的实验表明，ManiDreams在各种扰动下保持鲁棒性能，而RL基线显著退化。在推动、抓取、捕捉等任务和实际部署中展示了跨不同策略、优化器、物理后端和执行器的灵活性。

Conclusion: ManiDreams提供了一个实用的不确定性感知操作规划框架，通过将不确定性集成到规划循环中增强鲁棒性，无需重新训练基础策略，并在开源社区中提供了可运行的示例。

Abstract: Dynamics models, whether simulators or learned world models, have long been central to robotic manipulation, but most focus on minimizing prediction error rather than confronting a more fundamental challenge: real-world manipulation is inherently uncertain. We argue that robust manipulation under uncertainty is fundamentally an integration problem: uncertainties must be represented, propagated, and constrained within the planning loop, not merely suppressed during training. We present and open-source ManiDreams, a modular framework for uncertainty-aware manipulation planning over intuitive physics models. It realizes this integration through composable abstractions for distributional state representation, backend-agnostic dynamics prediction, and declarative constraint specification for action optimization. The framework explicitly addresses three sources of uncertainty: perceptual, parametric, and structural. It wraps any base policy with a sample-predict-constrain loop that evaluates candidate actions against distributional outcomes, adding robustness without retraining. Experiments on ManiSkill tasks show that ManiDreams maintains robust performance under various perturbations where the RL baseline degrades significantly. Runnable examples on pushing, picking, catching, and real-world deployment demonstrate flexibility across different policies, optimizers, physics backends, and executors. The framework is publicly available at https://github.com/Rice-RobotPI-Lab/ManiDreams

</details>


### [184] [Shifting Uncertainty to Critical Moments: Towards Reliable Uncertainty Quantification for VLA Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18342)
*Yanchuan Tang,Taowen Wang,Yuefei Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Qiang Guan,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于VLA模型的新型不确定性量化方法，通过最大滑动窗口池化、运动感知稳定性加权和自由度自适应校准来改进机器人策略的失败预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏可靠的内省能力，传统基于令牌级不确定性均值的方法会稀释连续控制中短暂但安全关键的不确定性峰值，导致失败预测不准确

Method: 1) 使用基于最大值的滑动窗口池化保留瞬态风险信号；2) 应用运动感知稳定性加权强调与不稳定行为相关的高频动作振荡；3) 通过贝叶斯优化进行自由度自适应校准，优先考虑运动学关键轴

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，该方法显著提高了失败预测准确率，并产生更可靠的失败检测信号，可支持下游人机交互干预

Conclusion: 提出的不确定性量化方法能有效捕捉VLA策略中的瞬态风险信号，为机器人安全监控和人机交互提供了更可靠的内省能力

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable general-purpose robotic policies by mapping visual observations and language instructions to low-level actions, but they often lack reliable introspection. A common practice is to compute a token-level uncertainty signal and take its mean over a rollout. However, mean aggregation can dilute short-lived but safety-critical uncertainty spikes in continuous control. In particular, successful rollouts may contain localized high-entropy segments due to benign noise or non-critical micro-adjustments, while failure rollouts can appear low-entropy for most timesteps and only exhibit brief spikes near the onset of failure. We propose a unified uncertainty quantification approach for predicting rollout success versus failure that (1) uses max-based sliding window pooling to preserve transient risk signals, (2) applies motion-aware stability weighting to emphasize high-frequency action oscillations associated with unstable behaviors, and (3) performs DoF-adaptive calibration via Bayesian Optimization to prioritize kinematically critical axes. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark show that our method substantially improves failure prediction accuracy and yields more reliable signals for failure detection, which can support downstream human-in-the-loop interventions.

</details>


### [185] [HRI-SA: A Multimodal Dataset for Online Assessment of Human Situational Awareness during Remote Human-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18344)
*Hashini Senaratne,Richard Attfield,Samith Widhanapathirana,David Howard,Cecile Paris,Dana Kulic,Leimin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HRI-SA数据集：首个支持人机团队情境感知在线评估的公开多模态数据集，包含眼动、生理信号等数据，用于检测情境感知延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高工作负荷和动态环境下，操作员常出现情境感知（SA）缺口。现有SA测量方法要么中断任务流程，要么无法捕捉实时波动，且缺乏公开数据集支持人机团队在线SA评估的系统研究。

Method: 构建HRI-SA多模态数据集：30名参与者在真实搜救人机团队场景中，收集眼动、瞳孔直径、生物信号、用户交互和机器人数据。实验协议包含预定义的需要及时操作员协助的事件，通过测量协助需求出现到解决的时间差，系统获取两种类型（感知和理解）的SA延迟真值。

Result: 使用通用眼动特征和上下文特征评估标准机器学习模型检测感知SA延迟的效果：仅眼动特征即可有效分类感知SA延迟（召回率=88.91%，F1=67.63%），通过上下文数据融合性能进一步提升（召回率=91.51%，F1=80.38%）。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了首个支持人机团队任务全过程中SA系统评估的公开数据集，同时证明了通用眼动特征在远程人机团队中连续检测感知SA延迟的潜力。

Abstract: Maintaining situational awareness (SA) is critical in human-robot teams. Yet, under high workload and dynamic conditions, operators often experience SA gaps. Automated detection of SA gaps could provide timely assistance for operators. However, conventional SA measures either disrupt task flow or cannot capture real-time fluctuations, limiting their operational utility. To the best of our knowledge, no publicly available dataset currently supports the systematic evaluation of online human SA assessment in human-robot teaming. To advance the development of online SA assessment tools, we introduce HRI-SA, a multimodal dataset from 30 participants in a realistic search-and-rescue human-robot teaming context, incorporating eye movements, pupil diameter, biosignals, user interactions, and robot data. The experimental protocol included predefined events requiring timely operator assistance, with ground truth SA latency of two types (perceptual and comprehension) systematically obtained by measuring the time between assistance need onset and resolution. We illustrate the utility of this dataset by evaluating standard machine learning models for detecting perceptual SA latencies using generic eye-tracking features and contextual features. Results show that eye-tracking features alone effectively classified perceptual SA latency (recall=88.91%, F1=67.63%) using leave-one-group-out cross-validation, with performance improved through contextual data fusion (recall=91.51%, F1=80.38%). This paper contributes the first public dataset supporting the systematic evaluation of SA throughout a human-robot teaming mission, while also demonstrating the potential of generic eye-tracking features for continuous perceptual SA latency detection in remote human-robot teaming.

</details>


### [186] [Multi-material Direct Ink Writing and Embroidery for Stretchable Wearable Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18354)
*Lukas Cha,Ryman Hashem,Ria Prakash,Tanguy Declety,Wenze Zhang,Liang He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合多材料直写打印与自动刺绣的织物兼容制造工艺，用于制造直接嵌入服装的可拉伸应变传感器，实现运动监测应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动表现跟踪、康复和损伤预防的可穿戴传感系统需求增长，需要结合舒适性、耐用性和精确运动检测的智能服装。

Method: 采用多材料直写打印与自动刺绣相结合的织物兼容制造流程：顺序打印硅胶-碳脂-硅胶堆叠层，然后通过自动刺绣实现机械固定和电气接口一体化。

Result: 混合传感器拉伸性达120%，60%应变内呈近似线性响应(R²=0.99)，应变系数31.4，滞后22.9%；80次循环测试显示基线和峰值漂移分别为0.135%和0.236%；硅胶-织物界面在大变形下保持完整；集成于肘部和膝部护套可监测关节角度。

Conclusion: 通过刺绣集成同时解决机械固定和电气接口问题，为将印刷可拉伸电子器件融入纺织品系统提供了可重复、可扩展的途径，适用于运动捕捉和软机器人应用。

Abstract: The development of wearable sensing systems for sports performance tracking, rehabilitation, and injury prevention has driven growing demand for smart garments that combine comfort, durability, and accurate motion detection. This paper presents a textile-compatible fabrication workflow that integrates multi-material direct ink writing with automated embroidery to create stretchable strain sensors directly embedded into garments. The process combines sequential multi-material printing of a silicone-carbon grease-silicone stack with automated embroidery that provides both mechanical fixation and electrical interfacing in a single step. The resulting hybrid sensor demonstrates stretchability up to 120% strain while maintaining electrical continuity, with approximately linear behaviour up to 60% strain (R^2 = 0.99), a gauge factor of 31.4, and hysteresis of 22.9%. Repeated loading-unloading tests over 80 cycles show baseline and peak drift of 0.135% and 0.236% per cycle, respectively, indicating moderate cycle-to-cycle stability. Mechanical testing further confirms that the silicone-fabric interface remains intact under large deformation, with failure occurring in the textile rather than at the stitched boundary. As a preliminary proof of concept, the sensor was integrated into wearable elbow and knee sleeves for joint angle monitoring, showing a clear correlation between normalised resistance change and bending angle. By addressing both mechanical fixation and electrical interfacing through embroidery-based integration, this approach provides a reproducible and scalable pathway for incorporating printed stretchable electronics into textile systems for motion capture and soft robotic applications.

</details>


### [187] [Contact Status Recognition and Slip Detection with a Bio-inspired Tactile Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18370)
*Chengxiao He,Wenhui Yang,Hongliang Zhao,Jiacheng Lv,Yuzhe Shao,Longhui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于多模态触觉反馈的机器人手滑移检测方法，通过分布式触觉传感器和接触状态识别实现高精度滑移检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中稳定可靠的抓取至关重要，特别是对于易碎和光滑物体。现有方法通常假设物体已被牢固抓取，但在非结构化环境中需要滑移检测和及时预防。传统阈值方法存在局限性，需要更智能的滑移检测方法。

Method: 使用五指仿生手的多模态触觉反馈，在软皮肤中嵌入分布式触觉传感元件（共24个通道）。将滑移检测问题转化为接触状态识别，结合分箱技术。对触觉信号进行离散小波变换，从不同时间和频带提取17个特征。使用最优120个特征进行状态识别。

Result: 在三种不同滑动速度和六种材料下，测试准确率达到96.39%。应用于四种未见材料时，仍获得91.95%的高准确率，验证了方法的泛化能力。基于训练好的接触状态识别模型验证了滑移检测性能。

Conclusion: 提出的基于多模态触觉反馈和接触状态识别的方法能够有效检测滑移，具有高准确率和良好的泛化能力，为机器人操作中的滑移预防提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Stable and reliable grasp is critical to robotic manipulations especially for fragile and glazed objects, where the grasp force requires precise control as too large force possibly damages the objects while small force leads to slip and fall-off. Although it is assumed the objects to manipulate is grasped firmly in advance, slip detection and timely prevention are necessary for a robot in unstructured and universal environments. In this work, we addressed this issue by utilizing multimodal tactile feedback from a five-fingered bio-inspired hand. Motivated by human hands, the tactile sensing elements were distributed and embedded into the soft skin of robotic hand, forming 24 tactile channels in total. Different from the threshold method that was widely employed in most existing works, we converted the slip detection problem to contact status recognition in combination with binning technique first and then detected the slip onset time according to the recognition results. After the 24-channel tactile signals passed through discrete wavelet transform, 17 features were extracted from different time and frequency bands. With the optimal 120 features employed for status recognition, the test accuracy reached 96.39% across three different sliding speeds and six kinds of materials. When applied to four new unseen materials, a high accuracy of 91.95% was still achieved, which further validated the generalization of our proposed method. Finally, the performance of slip detection is verified based on the trained model of contact status recognition.

</details>


### [188] [Graph-of-Constraints Model Predictive Control for Reactive Multi-agent Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18400)
*Anastasios Manganaris,Jeremy Lu,Ahmed H. Qureshi,Suresh Jagannathan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoC-MPC是一种用于多智能体任务与运动规划的图约束模型预测控制框架，支持部分有序任务、动态智能体协调和扰动恢复，仅通过视觉观测实现鲁棒的多智能体操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理几何约束序列的方法在处理部分有序任务和动态智能体分配方面存在局限，通常假设静态分配且无法适应扰动导致的任务分配变化。

Method: 提出图约束模型预测控制（GoC-MPC），将广义约束序列框架与MPC集成，通过跟踪3D关键点定义约束，无需训练数据或环境模型，仅通过视觉观测实现鲁棒控制。

Result: 实验表明GoC-MPC相比现有基线方法具有更高的成功率、显著更快的TAMP计算速度和更短的整体路径，成为现实世界扰动下多智能体操作的高效鲁棒解决方案。

Conclusion: GoC-MPC为多智能体TAMP问题提供了支持部分有序任务、动态协调和扰动恢复的通用框架，仅依赖视觉观测即可实现鲁棒的多智能体操作，在现实世界应用中表现出色。

Abstract: Sequences of interdependent geometric constraints are central to many multi-agent Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) problems. However, existing methods for handling such constraint sequences struggle with partially ordered tasks and dynamic agent assignments. They typically assume static assignments and cannot adapt when disturbances alter task allocations. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Graph-of-Constraints Model Predictive Control (GoC-MPC), a generalized sequence-of-constraints framework integrated with MPC. GoC-MPC naturally supports partially ordered tasks, dynamic agent coordination, and disturbance recovery.
